container of the nearest built first-parent ancestor
(scripts/select-qwen-container.sh), which for this branch is 2d294f0
(komoriyuta's gigatoken build, run 39126563684, image 317ab7c7), not
a8ed4592; provision-firmus-framework.sh then rejected the mismatch with the
pinned a8ed4592 before anything was staged.

Image 317ab7c7 is built from main at 2d294f0: the same SGLang patch chain,
DeepGEMM header series and router as a8ed4592 (main 7589c59) plus
gigatoken-encode.patch and the gigatoken package, which Firmus does not use
(configs/firmus-framework.json selects no tokenizer backend). The overlay
patches, add-rmsnorm-group-quant and prefill-attention-contiguous-kv, are
the ones image b8531630 applied on top of exactly this chain (gigatoken
included), so they apply as before. Nothing else changes from dfa99be.
